# The game's Capture the Flag rules (tools/engine_ctf_reference.py).
# game <n> <flag must reset> <flag at home to score> <score to win> <red stand x y> <blue stand x y>
#   (the stands and the players' places in quarter metres)
# join <team> <x> <y>: a player, standing there
# go <player> <x> <y>: where a player makes for now
# kill <tick> <player>: a player dies before the tick, dropping what it carries
# spawn <tick> <player> <x> <y>: a player comes back there before the tick
# state <tick> <red flag> <blue flag> <red score> <blue score> <each player's captures> <over>
#   (after the tick, when it changed: a flag is home, carried <player> or dropped <x> <y>)
# end <tick>: the game's last tick
